bus_id_to_idx,\n        'gen_bus_idx': gen_bus_idx,\n        'branch_from_idx': branch_from_idx,\n        'branch_to_idx': branch_to_idx,\n        'active_gens': active_gens,\n        'active_branches': active_branches,\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(Vm, Va, branches, branch_from_idx, branch_to_idx, active_branches, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute branch flows in MW/MVAr and MVA.\n\n    Returns list of dicts with flow info for each branch.\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n\n    for br_idx in range(len(branches)):\n        br = branches[br_idx]\n        from_bus = int(br[0])\n        to_bus = int(br[1])\n\n        if not active_branches[br_idx]:\n            results.append({\n                'from_bus': from_bus,\n                'to_bus': to_bus,\n                'P_ij_MW': 0.0,\n                'Q_ij_MVAr': 0.0,\n                'S_ij_MVA': 0.0,\n                'P_ji_MW': 0.0,\n                'Q_ji_MVAr': 0.0,\n                'S_ji_MVA': 0.0,\n                'rate_a_MVA': br[5],\n                'loading_pct': 0.0,\n            })\n            continue\n\n        i = branch_from_idx[br_idx]\n        j = branch_to_idx[br_idx]\n\n        r = br[2]\n        x = br[3]\n        b_c = br[4]\n        tap = br[8]\n        shift = br[9]\n        rate_a = br[5]\n\n        if abs(tap) < 1e-12:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        shift_rad = np.deg2rad(shift)\n\n        if abs(r) < 1e-12 and abs(x) < 1e-12:\n            g = 0.0\n            b = 0.0\n        else:\n            denom = r**2 + x**2\n            g = r / denom\n            b = -x / denom\n\n        inv_t = 1.0 / tap\n        inv_t2 = inv_t**2\n\n        Vi = Vm[i]\n        Vj = Vm[j]\n        theta_i = Va[i]\n        theta_j = Va[j]\n\n        delta_ij = theta_i - theta_j - shift_rad\n        delta_ji = theta_j - theta_i + shift_rad\n\n        P_ij = g * Vi**2 * inv_t2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (g * np.cos(delta_ij) + b * np.sin(delta_ij))\n        Q_ij = -(b + b_c/2) * Vi**2 * inv_t2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (g * np.sin(delta_ij) - b * np.cos(delta_ij))\n\n        P_ji = g * Vj**2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (g * np.cos(delta_ji) + b * np.sin(delta_ji))\n        Q_ji = -(b + b_c/2) * Vj**2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (g * np.sin(delta_ji) - b * np.cos(delta_ji))\n\n        S_ij = np.sqrt(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2)\n        S_ji = np.sqrt(P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2)\n\n        loading_pct = 0.0\n        if rate_a > 1e-6:\n            loading_pct = 100 * max(S_ij, S_ji) * baseMVA / rate_a\n\n        results.append({\n            'from_bus': from_bus,\n            'to_bus': to_bus,\n            'P_ij_MW': P_ij * baseMVA,\n            'Q_ij_MVAr': Q_ij * baseMVA,\n            'S_ij_MVA': S_ij * baseMVA,\n            'P_ji_MW': P_ji * baseMVA,\n            'Q_ji_MVAr': Q_ji * baseMVA,\n            'S_ji_MVA': S_ji * baseMVA,\n            'rate_a_MVA': rate_a,\n            'loading_pct': loading_pct,\n        })\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef compute_feasibility_check(network, solution, branch_flows):\n    \"\"\"\n    Check feasibility of solution.\n\n    Returns dict with max mismatches and violations.\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    buses = network['bus']\n    gens = network['gen']\n    branches = network['branch']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n    Pg = solution['Pg']\n    Qg = solution['Qg']\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = branch_from_idx = solution['branch_from_idx']\n    branch_to_idx = solution['branch_to_idx']\n\n    # Aggregate generation per bus\n    Pg_bus = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    Qg_bus = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    for Minimize total generation cost (quadratic)\n- Subject to: power balance, generator limits, voltage limits, branch flow limits\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport casadi as ca\n\ndef load_network(filepath):\n    \"\"\"Load network data from JSON.\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    return {\n        'baseMVA': float(data['baseMVA']),\n        'bus': np.array(data['bus'], dtype=float),\n        'gen': np.array(data['gen'], dtype=float),\n        'branch': np.array(data['branch'], dtype=float),\n        'gencost': np.array(data['gencost'], dtype=float),\n    }\n\ndef build_bus_id_to_idx(buses):\n    \"\"\"Create mapping from bus ID to 0-indexed position.\"\"\"\n    return {int(buses[i, 0]): i for i in range(len(buses))}\n\ndef solve_acopf(network):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve AC OPF problem.\n\n    Returns solution dict with:\n    - Vm, Va: voltage magnitude (pu) and angle (radians)\n    - Pg, Qg: generator real/reactive power (pu)\n    - objective: total cost ($/hr)\n    - status: solver status\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    buses = network['bus']\n    gens = network['gen']\n    branches = build_bus_id_to_idx(buses)\n\n    # Find slack bus (type 3)\n    slack_idx = None\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if buses[i, 1] == 3:\n            slack_idx = i\n            break\n\n    # Map generators to bus indices\n    gen_bus_idx = [bus_id_to_idx[int(g[0])] for g in gens]\n\n    # Filter to in-service generators\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7]  # GEN_STATUS\n    active_gens = gen_status == 1\n\n    # Filter to in-service branches\n    branch_status = branches[:, 10]  # BR_STATUS\n    active_branches = branch_status == 1\n\n    # Map branch endpoints\n    branch_from_idx = Voltage magnitudes (pu)\n    Va = ca.MX.sym(\"Va\", n_bus)   # Voltage angles (radians)\n    Pg = ca.MX.sym(\"Pg\", n_gen)   # Real power (pu)\n    Qg = ca.MX.sym(\"Qg\", n_gen)   # Reactive power (pu)\n\n    # Stack all decision variables\n    x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n    print(f\"DEBUG after vertcat: x.shape={x.shape}\")\n\n    # ========== Variable Bounds ==========\n    # Voltage magnitude bounds\n    Vm_min = buses[:, 12]  # VMIN\n    Vm_max = buses[:, 11]  # VMAX\n\n    # Voltage angle bounds (typically +/- pi)\n    Va_min = np.full(n_bus, -np.pi)\n    Va_max = np.full(n_bus, np.pi)\n    # Fix slack bus angle to 0\n    if slack_idx is not None:\n        Va_min[slack_idx] = 0.0\n        Va_max[slack_idx] = 0.0\n\n    # Generator bounds (convert to per-unit)\n    Pg_min = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    Pg_max = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    Qg_min = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    Qg_max = np.zeros(n_gen)\n\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        if active_gens[k]:\n            Pg_min[k] = gens[k, 9] / baseMVA  # PMIN\n            Pg_max[k] = gens[k, 8] / baseMVA  # PMAX\n            Qg_min[k] = gens[k, 4] / baseMVA  # QMIN\n            Qg_max[k] = gens[k, 3] / baseMVA  # QMAX\n        else:\n            # Out-of-service: fix to 0\n            Pg_min[k] = 0.0\n            Pg_max[k] = 0.0\n            Qg_min[k] = 0.0\n            Qg_max[k] = 0.0\n\n    lbx = np.concatenate([Vm_min, Va_min, Pg_min, Qg_min]).tolist()\n    ubx = np.concatenate([Vm_max, Va_max, Pg_max, Qg_max]).tolist()\n\n    # ========== Objective Function ==========\n    # Quadratic cost: c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0\n    # gencost format: [MODEL, STARTUP, SHUTDOWN, NCOST, c2, c1, c0] for polynomial\n    obj = ca.MX(0)\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        if active_gens[k]:\n            # Convert Pg from pu to MW for cost function\n            Pg_MW = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n            c2 = gencost[k, 4]  # $/MW^2\n            c1 = gencost[k, 5]  # $/MW\n            c0 = gencost[k, 6]  # $\n            obj += c2 * Pg_MW**2 + c1 * Pg_MW + c0\n\n    # ========== Constraints ==========\n    g_expr = []\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n\n    # ----- Power Balance Constraints -----\n    # For each bus: sum(Pg) - sum(Pd) - Gs*|V|^2 = sum(P_branch_out)\n    # For each bus: sum(Qg) - sum(Qd) + Bs*|V|^2 = sum(Q_branch_out)\n\n    # Aggregate generator power per bus\n    Pg_bus = [ca.MX(0) for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    Qg_bus = [ca.MX(0) for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        if active_gens[k]:\n            bus_idx = gen_bus_idx[k]\n            Pg_bus[bus_idx] += Pg[k]\n            Qg_bus[bus_idx] += Qg[k]\n\n    # Load at each bus (convert to pu)\n    Pd_bus = buses[:, 2] / baseMVA  # PD\n    Qd_bus = buses[:, 3] / baseMVA  # QD\n\n    # Shunt admittance at each bus (pu)\n    Gs_bus = buses[:, 4] / baseMVA  # GS (conductance in MW at V=1)\n    Bs_bus = buses[:, 5] / baseMVA  # BS (susceptance in MVAr at V=1)\n\n    # Compute branch flows and aggregate per bus\n    P_out = [ca.MX(0) for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    Q_out = [ca.MX(0) for _ in range(n_bus)]\n\n    # Store symbolic branch flows for limit constraints\n    S_ij_list = []  # |S| from i->j\n    S_ji_list = []  # |S| from j->i\n\n    for br_idx in range(n_branch):\n        if not active_branches[br_idx]:\n            S_ij_list.append(ca.MX(0))\n            S_ji_list.append(ca.MX(0))\n            continue\n\n line charging)\n\n        tap = br[8]   # TAP\n        shift = br[9] # SHIFT (degrees)\n\n        # Handle tap = 0 means 1.0\n        if abs(tap) < 1e-12:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        shift_rad = np.deg2rad(shift)\n\n        # Series admittance\n        if abs(br_r) < 1e-12 and abs(br_x) < 1e-12:\n            gs = 0.0\n            bs = 0.0\n        else:\n            denom = br_r**2 + br_x**2\n            gs = br_r / denom\n            bs = -br_x / denom\n\n        inv_t = 1.0 / tap\n        inv_t2 = inv_t**2\n\n        # Voltage variables\n        Vi = Vm[i]\n        Vj = Vm[j]\n        theta_i = Va[i]\n        theta_j = Va[j]\n\n        # Angle differences\n        delta_ij = theta_i - theta_j - shift_rad\n        delta_ji = theta_j - theta_i + shift_rad\n\n        # Power flows (per-unit)\n        # P_ij = g*Vi^2*inv_t2 - Vi*Vj*inv_t*(g*cos(delta_ij) + b*sin(delta_ij))\n        P_ij = gs * Vi**2 * inv_t2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * ca.cos(delta_ij) + bs * ca.sin(delta_ij))\n        # Q_ij = -(b + b_c/2)*Vi^2*inv_t2 - Vi*Vj*inv_t*(g*sin(delta_ij) - b*cos(delta_ij))\n        Q_ij = -(bs + b_c/2) * Vi**2 * inv_t2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * ca.sin(delta_ij) - bs * ca.cos(delta_ij))\n\n        # P_ji = g*Vj^2 - Vi*Vj*inv_t*(g*cos(delta_ji) + b*sin(delta_ji))\n        P_ji = gs * Vj**2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * ca.cos(delta_ji) + bs * ca.sin(delta_ji))\n        # Q_ji = -(b + b_c/2)*Vj^2 - Vi*Vj*inv_t*(g*sin(delta_ji) - b*cos(delta_ji))\n        Q_ji = -(bs + b_c/2) * Vj**2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * ca.sin(delta_ji) - bs * ca.cos(delta_ji))\n\n        # Aggregate for nodal balance\n        P_out[i] += P_ij\n        Q_out[i] += Q_ij\n        P_out[j] += P_ji\n        Q_out[j] += Q_ji\n\n        # Apparent power magnitudes\n        S_ij = ca.sqrt(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2)\n        S_ji = ca.sqrt(P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2)\n        S_ij_list.append(S_ij)\n        S_ji_list.append(S_ji)\n\n    # Nodal balance constraints\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Real power balance\n        P_balance = Pg_bus[i] - Pd_bus[i] - Gs_bus[i] * Vm[i]**2 - P_out[i]\n        g_expr.append(P_balance)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # Reactive power balance\n        Q_balance = Qg_bus[i] - Qd_bus[i] + Bs_bus[i] * Vm[i]**2 - Q_out[i]\n        g_expr.append(Q_balance)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # ----- Branch Flow Limits -----\n    for br_idx in range(n_branch):\n        if not active_branches[br_idx]:\n            continue\n\n        rate_a = branches[br_idx, 5] / baseMVA  # RATE_A in pu\n\n        if rate_a > 1e-6:  # Only constrain if limit exists\n            # |S_ij| <= rate_a\n            g_expr.append(S_ij_list[br_idx])\n            lbg.append(-ca.inf)\n            ubg.append(rate_a)\n\n            # |S_ji| <= rate_a\n            g_expr.append(S_ji_list[br_idx])\n            lbg.append(-ca.inf)\n            ubg.append(rate_a)\n\n    # ----- Angle Difference Constraints -----\n    for br_idx in range(n_branch):\n        if not active_branches[br_idx]:\n            continue\n\n        br = branches[br_idx]\n        i = branch_from_idx[br_idx]\n        j = branch_to_idx[br_idx]\n\n        ang_min = np.deg2rad(br[11])  # ANGMIN\n        ang_max = np.deg2rad(br[12])  # ANGMAX\n\n        angle_diff = Va[i] - Va[j]\n        g_expr.append(angle_diff)\n        lbg.append(ang_min)\n        ubg.append(ang_max)\n\n    g = ca.vertcat(*g_expr)\n\n    # DEBUG: Check symbolic status\n    print(f\"DEBUG: x.shape={x.shape}, f.shape={obj.shape}, g.shape={g.shape}\")\n    print(f\"DEBUG: len(lbx)={len(lbx)}, len(ubx)={len(ubx)}\")\n    print(f\"DEBUG: len(lbg)={len(lbg)}, len(ubg)={len(ubg)}\")\n    print(f\"DEBUG: x is_valid: {x.is_valid()}, g is_valid: {g.is_valid()}, obj is_valid: {obj.is_valid()}\")\n\n    # ========== Initialization ==========\n    # Use flat start: Vm=1.0, Va=0.0\n    Vm_init = np.ones(n_bus)\n    Va_init = np.zeros(n_bus)\n\n    # Initialize generators from data (clipped to bounds)\n    Pg_init = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    Qg_init = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        if active_gens[k]:\n            Pg_init[k] = np.clip(gens[k, 1] / baseMVA, Pg_min[k], Pg_max[k])\n            Qg_init[k] = np.clip(gens[k, 2] / baseMVA, Qg_min[k], Qg_max[k])\n\n    x0 = np.concatenate([Vm_init, Va_init, Pg_init, Qg_init]).tolist()\n\n    # ========== Create Solver ==========\n    nlp = {\"x\": x, \"f\": obj, \"g\": g}\n    opts = {\n        \"ipopt.print_level\": 0,\n        \"ipopt.max_iter\": 2000,\n        \"ipopt.tol\": 1e-7,\n        \"ipopt.acceptable_tol\": 1e-5,\n        \"ipopt.mu_strategy\": \"adaptive\",\n        \"print_time\": False,\n    }\n    solver = ca.nlpsol(\"solver\", \"ipopt\", nlp, opts)\n\n    # ========== Solve ==========\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n    # Check solver status\n    stats = solver.stats()\n    status = stats.get('return_status', 'unknown')\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = np.array(sol[\"x\"]).flatten()\n    Vm_sol = x_opt[:n_bus]\n    Va_sol = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    Pg_sol = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Qg_sol = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:]\n\n bus_id_to_idx,\n        'gen_bus_idx': gen_bus_idx,\n        'branch_from_idx': branch_from_idx,\n        'branch_to_idx': branch_to_idx,\n        'active_gens': active_gens,\n        'active_branches': active_branches,\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(Vm, Va, branches, branch_from_idx, branch_to_idx, active_branches, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute branch flows in MW/MVAr and MVA.\n\n    Returns list of dicts with flow info for each branch.\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n\n    for br_idx in range(len(branches)):\n        br = branches[br_idx]\n        from_bus =    'P_ij_MW': 0.0,\n                'Q_ij_MVAr': 0.0,\n                'S_ij_MVA': 0.0,\n                'P_ji_MW': 0.0,\n                'Q_ji_MVAr': 0.0,\n                'S_ji_MVA': 0.0,\n                'rate_a_MVA': br[5],\n                'loading_pct': 0.0,\n            })\n            continue\n\n        i = branch_from_idx[br_idx]\n        j = branch_to_idx[br_idx]\n\n        r = br[2]\n        x = br[3]\n        b_c = br[4]\n        tap = br[8]\n        shift = br[9]\n        rate_a = br[5]\n\n        if abs(tap) < 1e-12:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        shift_rad = np.deg2rad(shift)\n\n        if abs(r) < 1e-12 and abs(x) < 1e-12:\n            g = 0.0\n            b = 0.0\n        else:\n            denom = r**2 + x**2\n            g = r / denom\n            b = -x / denom\n\n        inv_t = 1.0 / tap\n        inv_t2 = inv_t**2\n\n        Vi = Vm[i]\n        Vj = Vm[j]\n        theta_i = Va[i]\n        theta_j = Va[j]\n\n        delta_ij = theta_i - theta_j - shift_rad\n        delta_ji = theta_j - theta_i + shift_rad\n\n        P_ij = g * Vi**2 * inv_t2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (g * np.cos(delta_ij) + b * np.sin(delta_ij))\n        Q_ij = -(b + b_c/2) * Vi**2 * inv_t2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (g * np.sin(delta_ij) - b * np.cos(delta_ij))\n\n        P_ji = g * Vj**2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (g * np.cos(delta_ji) + b * np.sin(delta_ji))\n        Q_ji = -(b + b_c/2) * Vj**2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (g * np.sin(delta_ji) - b * np.cos(delta_ji))\n\n        S_ij = np.sqrt(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2)\n        S_ji = np.sqrt(P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2)\n\n        loading_pct = 0.0\n        if rate_a > 1e-6:\n            loading_pct = 100 * max(S_ij, S_ji) * baseMVA / rate_a\n\n        results.append({\n            'from_bus': from_bus,\n            'to_bus': to_bus,\n            'P_ij_MW': P_ij * baseMVA,\n            'Q_ij_MVAr': Q_ij * baseMVA,\n            'S_ij_MVA': S_ij * baseMVA,\n            'P_ji_MW': P_ji * baseMVA,\n            'Q_ji_MVAr': Q_ji * baseMVA,\n            'S_ji_MVA': S_ji * baseMVA,\n            'rate_a_MVA': rate_a,\n            'loading_pct': loading_pct,\n        })\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef compute_feasibility_check(network, solution, branch_flows):\n    \"\"\"\n    Check feasibility of solution.\n\n    Returns dict with max mismatches and violations.\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    buses = network['bus']\n    gens = network['gen']\n    branches = network['branch']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n    Pg = solution['Pg']\n    Qg = solution['Qg']\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n\n    bus_id_to_idx = solution['bus_id_to_idx']\n    gen_bus_idx = solution['gen_bus_idx']\n    active_gens = solution['active_gens']\n    active_branches = solution['active_branches']\n    P_out[i] += P_ij\n        Q_out[i] += Q_ij\n        P_out[j] += P_ji\n        Q_out[j] += Q_ji\n\n        # Apparent power magnitudes\n        S_ij = ca.sqrt(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2)\n        S_ji = ca.sqrt(P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2)\n        S_ij_list.append(S_ij)\n        S_ji_list.append(S_ji)\n\n    # Nodal balance constraints\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Real power balance\n        P_balance = Pg_bus[i] - Pd_bus[i] - Gs_bus[i] * Vm[i]**2 - P_out[i]\n        g_expr.append(P_balance)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # Reactive power balance\n        Q_balance = Qg_bus[i] - Qd_bus[i] + Bs_bus[i] * Vm[i]**2 - Q_out[i]\n        g_expr.append(Q_balance)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # ----- Branch Flow Limits -----\n    for br_idx in range(n_branch):\n        if not active_branches[br_idx]:\n            continue\n\n        rate_a = branches[br_idx, 5] / baseMVA  # RATE_A in pu\n\n        if rate_a > 1e-6:  # Only constrain if limit exists\n            # |S_ij| <= rate_a\n            g_expr.append(S_ij_list[br_idx])\n            lbg.append(-ca.inf)\n            ubg.append(rate_a)\n\n            # |S_ji| <= rate_a\n            g_expr.append(S_ji_list[br_idx])\n            lbg.append(-ca.inf)\n            ubg.append(rate_a)\n\n    # ----- Angle Difference Constraints -----\n    for br_idx in range(n_branch):\n        if not active_branches[br_idx]:\n            continue\n\n        br = branches[br_idx]\n        i = branch_from_idx[br_idx]\n        j = branch_to_idx[br_idx]\n\n        ang_min = np.deg2rad(br[11])  # ANGMIN\n        ang_max = np.deg2rad(br[12])  # ANGMAX\n\n        angle_diff = Va[i] - Va[j]\n        g_expr.append(angle_diff)\n        lbg.append(ang_min)\n        ubg.append(ang_max)\n\n    g = ca.vertcat(*g_expr)\n\n    # DEBUG: Check symbolic status\n    print(f\"DEBUG: x.shape={x.shape}, f.shape={obj.shape}, g.shape={g.shape}\")\n    print(f\"DEBUG: len(lbx)={len(lbx)}, len(ubx)={len(ubx)}\")\n    print(f\"DEBUG: len(lbg)={len(lbg)}, len(ubg)={len(ubg)}\")\n    print(f\"DEBUG: x is_valid: {x.is_valid()}, g is_valid: {g.is_valid()}, obj is_valid: {obj.is_valid()}\")\n\n    # ========== Initialization ==========\n    # Use flat start: Vm=1.0, Va=0.0\n    Vm_init = np.ones(n_bus)\n    Va_init = np.zeros(n_bus)\n\n    # Initialize generators from data (clipped to bounds)\n    Pg_init = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    Qg_init = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        if active_gens[k]:\n            Pg_init[k] = np.clip(gens[k, 1] / baseMVA, Pg_min[k], Pg_max[k])\n            Qg_init[k] = np.clip(gens[k, 2] / baseMVA, Qg_min[k], Qg_max[k])\n\n    x0 = np.concatenate([Vm_init, Va_init, Pg_init, Qg_init]).tolist()\n\n    # ========== Create Solver ==========\n    nlp = {\"x\": x, \"f\": obj, \"g\": g}\n    opts = {\n        \"ipopt.print_level\": 0,\n        \"ipopt.max_iter\": 2000,\n        \"ipopt.tol\": 1e-7,\n        \"ipopt.acceptable_tol\": 1e-5,\n        \"ipopt.mu_strategy\": \"adaptive\",\n        \"print_time\": False,\n    }\n    solver = ca.nlpsol(\"solver\", \"ipopt\", nlp, opts)\n\n    # ========== Solve ==========\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n    # Check solver status\n    stats = solver.stats()\n    status = stats.get('return_status', 'unknown')\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = np.array(sol[\"x\"]).flatten()\n    Vm_sol = x_opt[:n_bus]\n    Va_sol = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    Pg_sol = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Qg_sol = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:]\n\n    return {\n        'Vm': Vm_sol,\n        'Va': Va_sol,\n        'Pg': Pg_sol,\n        'Qg': Qg_sol,\n        'objective': float(sol[\"f\"]),\n        'status': status,\n        'bus_id_to_idx': bus_id_to_idx,\n        'gen_bus_idx': gen_bus_idx,\n        'branch_from_idx': branch_from_idx,\n        'branch_to_idx': branch_to_idx,\n        'active_gens': active_gens,\n        'active_branches': active_branches,\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(Vm, Va, branches, branch_from_idx, branch_to_idx, active_branches, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute branch flows in MW/MVAr and MVA.\n\n    Returns list of dicts with flow info for each branch.\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n\n    for br_idx in range(len(branches)):\n        br = branches[br_idx]\n        from_bus = int(br[0])\n        to_bus = int(br[1])\n\n        if not active_branches[br_idx]:\n            results.append({\n                'from_bus': from_bus,\n                'to_bus': to_bus,\n                'P_ij_MW': 0.0,\n                'Q_ij_MVAr': 0.0,\n                'S_ij_MVA': 0.0,\n                'P_ji_MW': 0.0,\n                'Q_ji_MVAr': 0.0,\n                'S_ji_MVA': 0.0,\n                'rate_a_MVA': br[5],\n                'loading_pct': 0.0,\n            })\n            continue\n\n        i = branch_from_idx[br_idx]\n        j = branch_to_idx[br_idx]\n\n        br_r = br[2]\n        br_x = br[3]\n        b_c = br[4]\n        tap = br[8]\n        shift = br[9]\n        rate_a = br[5]\n\n        if abs(tap) < 1e-12:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        shift_rad = np.deg2rad(shift)\n\n        if abs(br_r) < 1e-12 and abs(br_x) < 1e-12:\n            gs = 0.0\n            bs = 0.0\n        else:\n            denom = br_r**2 + br_x**2\n            gs = br_r / denom\n            bs = -br_x / denom\n\n        inv_t = 1.0 / tap\n        inv_t2 = inv_t**2\n\n        Vi = Vm[i]\n        Vj = Vm[j]\n        theta_i = Va[i]\n        theta_j = Va[j]\n\n        delta_ij = theta_i - theta_j - shift_rad\n        delta_ji = theta_j - theta_i + shift_rad\n\n        P_ij = gs * Vi**2 * inv_t2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * np.cos(delta_ij) + bs * np.sin(delta_ij))\n        Q_ij = -(bs + b_c/2) * Vi**2 * inv_t2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * np.sin(delta_ij) - bs * np.cos(delta_ij))\n\n        P_ji = gs * Vj**2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * np.cos(delta_ji) + bs * np.sin(delta_ji))\n        Q_ji = -(bs + b_c/2) * Vj**2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * np.sin(delta_ji) - bs * np.cos(delta_ji))\n\n        S_ij = np.sqrt(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2)\n        S_ji = np.sqrt(P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2)\n\n        loading_pct = 0.0\n        if rate_a > 1e-6:\n            loading_pct = 100 * max(S_ij, S_ji) * baseMVA / rate_a\n\n        results.append({\n            'from_bus': from_bus,\n            'to_bus': to_bus,\n            'P_ij_MW': P_ij * baseMVA,\n            'Q_ij_MVAr': Q_ij * baseMVA,\n            'S_ij_MVA': S_ij * baseMVA,\n            'P_ji_MW': P_ji * baseMVA,\n            'Q_ji_MVAr': Q_ji * baseMVA,\n            'S_ji_MVA': S_ji * baseMVA,\n            'rate_a_MVA': rate_a,\n            'loading_pct': loading_pct,\n        })\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef compute_feasibility_check(network, solution, branch_flows):\n    \"\"\"\n    Check feasibility of solution.\n\n    Returns dict with max mismatches and violations.\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    buses = network['bus']\n    gens = network['gen']\n    branches = network['branch']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n    Pg = solution['Pg']\n    Qg =    active_branches = solution['active_branches']\n    branch_from_idx = solution['branch_from_idx']\n    branch_to_idx = solution['branch_to_idx']\n\n    # Aggregate generation per bus\n    Pg_bus = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    Qg_bus = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        if active_gens[k]:\n            Pg_bus[gen_bus_idx[k]] += Pg[k]\n            Qg_bus[gen_bus_idx[k]] += Qg[k]\n\n    # Load and shunts\n    Pd_bus = buses[:, 2] / baseMVA\n    Qd_bus = buses[:, 3] / baseMVA\n    Gs_bus = buses[:, 4] / baseMVA\n    Bs_bus = buses[:, 5] / baseMVA\n\n    # Compute branch outflows\n    P_out = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    Q_out = np.zeros(n_bus)\n\n    for br_idx, br in enumerate(branches):\n        if not active_branches[br_idx]:\n            continue\n\n        i = branch_from_idx[br_idx]\n        j = branch_to_idx[br_idx]\n\n        P_out[i] += branch_flows[br_idx]['P_ij_MW'] / baseMVA\n        Q_out[i] += branch_flows[br_idx]['Q_ij_MVAr'] / baseMVA\n        P_out[j] += branch_flows[br_idx]['P_ji_MW'] / baseMVA\n        Q_out[j] += branch_flows[br_idx]['Q_ji_MVAr'] / baseMVA\n\n    # Power balance mismatches\n    P_mismatch = Pg_bus - Pd_bus - Gs_bus * Vm**2 - P_out\n    Q_mismatch = Qg_bus - Qd_bus + Bs_bus * Vm**2 - Q_out\n\n    max_p_mismatch = np.max(np.abs(P_mismatch)) * baseMVA\n    max_q_mismatch = np.max(np.abs(Q_mismatch)) * baseMVA\n\n    # Voltage violations\n    Vm_min = buses[:, 12]\n    Vm_max = buses[:, 11]\n    v_violation_low = np.maximum(0, Vm_min - Vm)\n    v_violation_high = np.maximum(0, Vm - Vm_max)\n    max_v_violation = range(n_bus):\n        if buses[i, 1] == 3:\n            slack_idx = i\n            break\n\n    # Map generators to bus indices\n    gen_bus_idx = [bus_id_to_idx[int(g[0])] for g in gens]\n\n    # Filter to in-service generators\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7]  # GEN_STATUS\n    active_gens = gen_status == 1\n\n    # Filter to in-service branches\n    branch_status = branches[:, 10]  # BR_STATUS\n    active_branches = branch_status == 1\n\n    # Map branch endpoints\n    branch_from_idx = [bus_id_to_idx[int(b[0])] for b in branches]\n    branch_to_idx = [bus_id_to_idx[int(b[1])] for b in branches]\n\n    # ========== Decision Variables ==========\n    Vm = ca.MX.sym(\"Vm\", n_bus)   # Voltage magnitudes (pu)\n    Va = ca.MX.sym(\"Va\", n_bus)   # Voltage angles (radians)\n    Pg = ca.MX.sym(\"Pg\", n_gen)   # Real power (pu)\n    Qg = ca.MX.sym(\"Qg\", n_gen)   # Reactive power (pu)\n\n    # Stack all decision variables\n    x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n    # ========== Variable Bounds ==========\n    # Voltage magnitude bounds\n    Vm_min = buses[:, 12]  # VMIN\n    Vm_max = buses[:, 11]  # VMAX\n\n    # Voltage angle bounds (typically +/- pi)\n    Va_min = np.full(n_bus, -np.pi)\n    Va_max = np.full(n_bus, np.pi)\n    # Fix slack bus angle to 0\n    if slack_idx is not None:\n        Va_min[slack_idx] = 0.0\n        Va_max[slack_idx] = 0.0\n\n    # Generator bounds (convert to per-unit)\n    Pg_min = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    Pg_max = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    Qg_min = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    Qg_max = np.zeros(n_gen)\n\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        if active_gens[k]:\n            Pg_min[k] = gens[k, 9] / baseMVA  # PMIN\n            Pg_max[k] = gens[k, 8] / baseMVA  # PMAX\n            Qg_min[k] = gens[k, 4] / baseMVA  # QMIN\n            Qg_max[k] = gens[k, 3] / baseMVA  # QMAX\n        else:\n            # Out-of-service: fix to 0\n            Pg_min[k] = 0.0\n            Pg_max[k] = 0.0\n            Qg_min[k] = 0.0\n            Qg_max[k] = 0.0\n\n    lbx = np.concatenate([Vm_min, Va_min, Pg_min, Qg_min]).tolist()\n    ubx = np.concatenate([Vm_max, Va_max, Pg_max, Qg_max]).tolist()\n\n    # ========== Objective Function ==========\n    # Quadratic cost: c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0\n    # gencost format: [MODEL, STARTUP, SHUTDOWN, NCOST, c2, c1, c0] for polynomial\n    obj = ca.MX(0)\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        if active_gens[k]:\n            # Convert Pg from pu to MW for cost function\n            Pg_MW = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n            c2 = gencost[k, 4]  # $/MW^2\n            c1 = gencost[k, 5]  # $/MW\n            c0 = gencost[k, 6]  # $\n            obj += c2 * Pg_MW**2 + c1 * Pg_MW + c0\n\n    # ========== Constraints ==========\n    g_expr = []\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n\n    # ----- Power Balance Constraints -----\n    # For each bus: sum(Pg) - sum(Pd) - Gs*|V|^2 = sum(P_branch_out)\n    # For each bus: sum(Qg) - sum(Qd) + Bs*|V|^2 = sum(Q_branch_out)\n\n    # Aggregate generator power per bus\n    Pg_bus = [ca.MX(0) for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    Qg_bus = [ca.MX(0) for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        if active_gens[k]:\n            bus_idx = gen_bus_idx[k]\n            Pg_bus[bus_idx] += Pg[k]\n            Qg_bus[bus_idx] += Qg[k]\n\n    # Load at each bus (convert to pu)\n    Pd_bus = buses[:, 2] / baseMVA  # PD\n    Qd_bus = buses[:, 3] / baseMVA  # QD\n\n    # Shunt admittance at each bus (pu)\n    Gs_bus = buses[:, 4] / baseMVA  # GS (conductance in MW at V=1)\n    Bs_bus = buses[:, 5] / baseMVA  # BS (susceptance in MVAr at V=1)\n\n    # Compute branch flows and aggregate per bus\n    P_out = [ca.MX(0) for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    Q_out = [ca.MX(0) for _ in range(n_bus)]\n\n    # Store symbolic branch flows for limit constraints\n    S_ij_list = []  # |S| from i->j\n    S_ji_list = []  # |S| from j->i\n\n    for br_idx in range(n_branch):\n        if not active_branches[br_idx]:\n            S_ij_list.append(ca.MX(0))\n            S_ji_list.append(ca.MX(0))\n            continue\n\n        br = branches[br_idx]\n        i = branch_from_idx[br_idx]\n        j = branch_to_idx[br_idx]\n\n        br_r = br[2]   # BR_R\n        br_x = br[3]   # BR_X\n        b_c = br[4] # BR_B (total line charging)\n\n        tap = br[8]   # TAP\n        shift = br[9] # SHIFT (degrees)\n\n        # Handle tap = 0 means 1.0\n        if abs(tap) < 1e-12:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        shift_rad = np.deg2rad(shift)\n\n        # Series admittance\n        if abs(br_r) < 1e-12 and abs(br_x) < 1e-12:\n            gs = 0.0\n            bs = 0.0\n        else:\n            denom = br_r**2 + br_x**2\n            gs = br_r / denom\n            bs = -br_x / denom\n\n        inv_t = 1.0 / tap\n        inv_t2 = inv_t**2\n\n        # Voltage variables\n        Vi = Vm[i]\n        Vj = Vm[j]\n        theta_i = Va[i]\n        theta_j = Va[j]\n\n        # Angle differences\n        delta_ij = theta_i - theta_j - shift_rad\n        delta_ji = theta_j - theta_i + shift_rad\n\n        # Power flows (per-unit)\n        # P_ij = g*Vi^2*inv_t2 - Vi*Vj*inv_t*(g*cos(delta_ij) + b*sin(delta_ij))\n        P_ij = gs * Vi**2 * inv_t2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * ca.cos(delta_ij) + bs * ca.sin(delta_ij))\n        # Q_ij = -(b + b_c/2)*Vi^2*inv_t2 - Vi*Vj*inv_t*(g*sin(delta_ij) - b*cos(delta_ij))\n        Q_ij = -(bs + b_c/2) * Vi**2 * inv_t2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * ca.sin(delta_ij) - bs * ca.cos(delta_ij))\n\n        # P_ji = g*Vj^2 - Vi*Vj*inv_t*(g*cos(delta_ji) + b*sin(delta_ji))\n        P_ji = gs * Vj**2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * ca.cos(delta_ji) + bs * ca.sin(delta_ji))\n        # Q_ji = -(b + b_c/2)*Vj^2 - Vi*Vj*inv_t*(g*sin(delta_ji) - b*cos(delta_ji))\n        Q_ji = -(bs + b_c/2) * Vj**2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * ca.sin(delta_ji) - bs * ca.cos(delta_ji))\n\n        # Aggregate for nodal balance\n        P_out[i] += P_ij\n        Q_out[i] += Q_ij\n        P_out[j] += P_ji\n        Q_out[j] += Q_ji\n\n        # Apparent power magnitudes\n        S_ij = ca.sqrt(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2)\n        S_ji = ca.sqrt(P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2)\n        S_ij_list.append(S_ij)\n        S_ji_list.append(S_ji)\n\n    # Nodal balance constraints\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Real power balance\n        P_balance = Pg_bus[i] - Pd_bus[i] - Gs_bus[i] * Vm[i]**2 - P_out[i]\n        g_expr.append(P_balance)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # Reactive power balance\n        Q_balance = Qg_bus[i] - Qd_bus[i] + Bs_bus[i] * Vm[i]**2 - Q_out[i]\n        g_expr.append(Q_balance)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # ----- Branch Flow Limits -----\n    for br_idx in range(n_branch):\n        if not active_branches[br_idx]:\n            continue\n\n        rate_a = branches[br_idx, 5] / baseMVA  # RATE_A in pu\n\n        if rate_a > 1e-6:  # Only constrain if limit exists\n            # |S_ij| <= rate_a\n            g_expr.append(S_ij_list[br_idx])\n            lbg.append(-ca.inf)\n            ubg.append(rate_a)\n\n            # |S_ji| <= rate_a\n            g_expr.append(S_ji_list[br_idx])\n            lbg.append(-ca.inf)\n            ubg.append(rate_a)\n\n    # ----- Angle Difference Constraints -----\n    for br_idx in range(n_branch):\n        if not active_branches[br_idx]:\n            continue\n\n        br = branches[br_idx]\n        i = branch_from_idx[br_idx]\n        j = branch_to_idx[br_idx]\n\n        ang_min = np.deg2rad(br[11])  # ANGMIN\n        ang_max = np.deg2rad(br[12])  # ANGMAX\n\n        angle_diff = Va[i] - Va[j]\n        g_expr.append(angle_diff)\n        lbg.append(ang_min)\n        ubg.append(ang_max)\n\n    g = ca.vertcat(*g_expr)\n\n    # DEBUG: Check symbolic status\n    print(f\"DEBUG: x.shape={x.shape}, f.shape={obj.shape}, g.shape={g.shape}\")\n    print(f\"DEBUG: len(lbx)={len(lbx)}, len(ubx)={len(ubx)}\")\n    print(f\"DEBUG: len(lbg)={len(lbg)}, len(ubg)={len(ubg)}\")\n    print(f\"DEBUG: x is_valid: {x.is_valid()}, g is_valid: {g.is_valid()}, obj is_valid: {obj.is_valid()}\")\n\n    # ========== Initialization ==========\n    # Use flat start: Vm=1.0, Va=0.0\n    Vm_init = np.ones(n_bus)\n    Va_init = np.zeros(n_bus)\n\n    # Initialize generators from data (clipped to bounds)\n    Pg_init = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    Qg_init = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        if active_gens[k]:\n            Pg_init[k] = np.clip(gens[k, 1] / baseMVA, Pg_min[k], Pg_max[k])\n            Qg_init[k] = np.clip(gens[k, 2] / baseMVA, Qg_min[k], Qg_max[k])\n\n    x0 = np.concatenate([Vm_init, Va_init, Pg_init, Qg_init]).tolist()\n\n    # ========== Create Solver ==========\n    nlp = {\"x\": x, \"f\": obj, \"g\": g}\n    opts = {\n        \"ipopt.print_level\": 0,\n        \"ipopt.max_iter\": 2000,\n        \"ipopt.tol\": 1e-7,\n        \"ipopt.acceptable_tol\": 1e-5,\n        \"ipopt.mu_strategy\": \"adaptive\",\n        \"print_time\": False,\n    }\n    solver = ca.nlpsol(\"solver\", \"ipopt\", nlp, opts)\n\n    # ========== Solve ==========\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n    # Check solver status\n    stats = solver.stats()\n    status = stats.get('return_status', 'unknown')\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = np.array(sol[\"x\"]).flatten()\n    Vm_sol = x_opt[:n_bus]\n    Va_sol = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    Pg_sol = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Qg_sol = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:]\n\n    return {\n        'Vm': Vm_sol,\n        'Va': Va_sol,\n        'Pg': Pg_sol,\n        'Qg': Qg_sol,\n        'objective': float(sol[\"f\"]),\n        'status': status,\n        'bus_id_to_idx': bus_id_to_idx,\n        'gen_bus_idx': gen_bus_idx,\n        'branch_from_idx': branch_from_idx,\n        'branch_to_idx': branch_to_idx,\n        'active_gens': active_gens,\n        'active_branches': active_branches,\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(Vm, Va, branches, branch_from_idx, branch_to_idx, active_branches, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute branch flows in MW/MVAr and MVA.\n\n    Returns list of dicts with flow info for each branch.\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n\n    for br_idx in range(len(branches)):\n        br = branches[br_idx]\n        from_bus = int(br[0])\n        to_bus = int(br[1])\n\n        if not active_branches[br_idx]:\n            results.append({\n                'from_bus': from_bus,\n                'to_bus': to_bus,\n                'P_ij_MW': 0.0,\n                'Q_ij_MVAr': 0.0,\n                'S_ij_MVA': 0.0,\n                'P_ji_MW': 0.0,\n                'Q_ji_MVAr': 0.0,\n                'S_ji_MVA': 0.0,\n                'rate_a_MVA': br[5],\n                'loading_pct': 0.0,\n            })\n            continue\n\n        i = branch_from_idx[br_idx]\n        j = branch_to_idx[br_idx]\n\n        br_r = br[2]\n        br_x = br[3]\n        b_c = br[4]\n        tap = br[8]\n        shift = br[9]\n        rate_a = br[5]\n\n        if abs(tap) < 1e-12:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        shift_rad = np.deg2rad(shift)\n\n        if abs(br_r) < 1e-12 and abs(br_x) < 1e-12:\n            gs = 0.0\n            bs = 0.0\n        else:\n            denom = br_r**2 + br_x**2\n            gs = br_r / denom\n            bs = -br_x / denom\n\n        inv_t = 1.0 / tap\n        inv_t2 = inv_t**2\n\n        Vi = Vm[i]\n        Vj = Vm[j]\n        theta_i = Va[i]\n        theta_j = Va[j]\n\n        delta_ij = theta_i - theta_j - shift_rad\n        delta_ji = theta_j - theta_i + shift_rad\n\n        P_ij = gs * Vi**2 * inv_t2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * np.cos(delta_ij) + bs * np.sin(delta_ij))\n        Q_ij = -(bs + b_c/2) * Vi**2 * inv_t2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * np.sin(delta_ij) - bs * np.cos(delta_ij))\n\n        P_ji = gs * Vj**2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * np.cos(delta_ji) + bs * np.sin(delta_ji))\n        Q_ji = -(bs + b_c/2) * Vj**2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * np.sin(delta_ji) - bs * np.cos(delta_ji))\n\n        S_ij = np.sqrt(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2)\n        S_ji = np.sqrt(P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2)\n\n        loading_pct = 0.0\n        if rate_a > 1e-6:\n            loading_pct = 100 * max(S_ij, S_ji) * baseMVA / rate_a\n\n        results.append({\n            'from_bus': from_bus,\n            'to_bus': to_bus,\n            'P_ij_MW': P_ij * baseMVA,\n            'Q_ij_MVAr': Q_ij * baseMVA,\n            'S_ij_MVA': S_ij * baseMVA,\n            'P_ji_MW': P_ji * baseMVA,\n            'Q_ji_MVAr': Q_ji * baseMVA,\n            'S_ji_MVA': S_ji * baseMVA,\n            'rate_a_MVA': rate_a,\n            'loading_pct': loading_pct,\n        })\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef compute_feasibility_check(network, solution, branch_flows):\n    \"\"\"\n    Check feasibility of solution.\n\n    Returns dict with max mismatches and violations.\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    buses = network['bus']\n    gens = network['gen']\n    branches = network['branch']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n    Pg = solution['Pg']\n    Qg = solution['Qg']\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n\n    bus_id_to_idx = solution['bus_id_to_idx']\n    gen_bus_idx = solution['gen_bus_idx']\n    active_gens = solution['active_gens']\n    active_branches = solution['active_branches']\n    branch_from_idx = solution['branch_from_idx']\n    branch_to_idx = solution['branch_to_idx']\n\n    # Aggregate generation per bus\n    Pg_bus = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    Qg_bus = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        if active_gens[k]:\n            Pg_bus[gen_bus_idx[k]] += Pg[k]\n            Qg_bus[gen_bus_idx[k]] += Qg[k]\n\n    # Load and shunts\n    Pd_bus = buses[:, 2] / baseMVA\n    Qd_bus = buses[:, 3] / baseMVA\n    Gs_bus = buses[:, 4] / baseMVA\n    Bs_bus = buses[:, 5] / baseMVA\n\n    # Compute branch outflows\n    P_out = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    Q_out = np.zeros(n_bus)\n\n    for br_idx, br in enumerate(branches):\n        if not active_branches[br_idx]:\n            continue\n\n        i = branch_from_idx[br_idx]\n        j = branch_to_idx[br_idx]\n\n        P_out[i] += branch_flows[br_idx]['P_ij_MW'] / baseMVA\n        Q_out[i] += branch_flows[br_idx]['Q_ij_MVAr'] / baseMVA\n        P_out[j] += branch_flows[br_idx]['P_ji_MW'] / baseMVA\n        Q_out[j] += branch_flows[br_idx]['Q_ji_MVAr'] / baseMVA\n\n    # Power balance mismatches\n    P_mismatch = Pg_bus - Pd_bus - Gs_bus * Vm**2 - P_out\n    Q_mismatch = Qg_bus - Qd_bus + Bs_bus * Vm**2 - Q_out\n\n    max_p_mismatch = np.max(np.abs(P_mismatch)) * baseMVA\n    max_q_mismatch = np.max(np.abs(Q_mismatch)) * baseMVA\n\n    # Voltage violations\n    Vm_min = buses[:, 12]\n    Vm_max = buses[:, 11]\n    v_violation_low = np.maximum(0, Vm_min - Vm)\n    v_violation_high = np.maximum(0, Vm - Vm_max)\n    max_v_violation = max(np.max(v_violation_low), np.max(v_violation_high))\n\n    # Branch overloads\n    max_overload = 0.0\n    for bf in branch_flows:\n        if bf['rate_a_MVA'] > 1e-6:\n            overload_ij = max(0, bf['S_ij_MVA'] - bf['rate_a_MVA'])\n            overload_ji = max(0, bf['S_ji_MVA'] - bf['rate_a_MVA'])\n            max_overload = max(max_overload, overload_ij, overload_ji)\n\n    return {\n        'max_p_mismatch_MW': max_p_mismatch,\n        'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': max_q_mismatch,\n        'max_voltage_violation_pu': max_v_violation,\n        'max_branch_overload_MVA': max_overload,\n    }\n\n\ndef generate_report(network, solution, branch_flows, feasibility):\n    \"\"\"\n    Generate report in the required JSON format.\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    buses = network['bus']\n    gens = network['gen']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n    Pg = solution['Pg']\n    Qg = solution['Qg']\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n\n    active_gens = solution['active_gens']\n    gen_bus_idx = solution['gen_bus_idx']\n    bus_id_to_idx = solution['bus_id_to_idx']\n\n    # Total load\n    total_load_MW = np.sum(buses[:, 2])\n    total_load_MVAr = np.sum(buses[:, 3])\n\n    # Total generation\n    total_gen_MW = np.sum(Pg) * baseMVA\n    total_gen_MVAr = np.sum(Qg) * baseMVA\n\n    # Total losses\n    total_losses_MW = total_gen_MW - total_load_MW\n\n    # Summary\n    summary = {\n        \"total_cost_per_hour\": round(solution['objective'], 2),\n        \"total_load_MW\": round(total_load_MW, 2),\n        \"total_load_MVAr\": round(total_load_MVAr, 2),\n        \"total_generation_MW\": round(total_gen_MW, 2),\n        \"total_generation_MVAr\": round(total_gen_MVAr, 2),\n        \"total_losses_MW\": round(total_losses_MW, 2),\n        \"solver_status\": solution['status'],\n    }\n\n    # Generators\n    generators = []\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        bus_idx = gen_bus_idx[k]\n        bus_id = int(gens[k, 0])\n        generators.append({\n            \"id\": k + 1,\n            \"bus\": bus_id,\n            \"pg_MW\": round(Pg[k] * baseMVA, 2),\n            \"qg_MVAr\": round(Qg[k] * baseMVA, 2),\n            \"pmin_MW\": round(gens[k, 9], 2),\n            \"pmax_MW\": round(gens[k, 8], 2),\n            \"qmin_MVAr\": round(gens[k, 4], 2),\n            \"qmax_MVAr\": round(gens[k, 3], 2),\n        })\n\n    # Buses\n    bus_list = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        bus_id = int(buses[i, 0])\n        bus_list.append({\n            \"id\": bus_id,\n            \"vm_pu\": round(Vm[i], 6),\n            \"va_deg\": round(np.rad2deg(Va[i]), 2),\n            \"vmin_pu\": round(buses[i, 12], 2),\n            \"vmax_pu\": round(buses[i, 11], 2),\n        })\n\n    # Most loaded branches (top 10)\n    # Filter branches with valid limits\n    branches_with_limits = [(i, bf) for i, bf in enumerate(branch_flows) if bf['rate_a_MVA'] > 1e-6]\n    branches_sorted = sorted(branches_with_limits, key=lambda x: x[1]['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n\n    most_loaded = []\n    for i, bf in branches_sorted[:10]:\n        most_loaded.append({\n            \"from_bus\": bf['from_bus'],\n            \"to_bus\": bf['to_bus'],\n            \"loading_pct\": round(bf['loading_pct'], 2),\n            \"flow_from_MVA\": round(bf['S_ij_MVA'], 2),\n            \"flow_to_MVA\": round(bf['S_ji_MVA'], 2),\n            \"limit_MVA\": round(bf['rate_a_MVA'], 2),\n        })\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    feasibility_check = {\n        \"max_p_mismatch_MW\": round(feasibility['max_p_mismatch_MW'], 6),\n        \"max_q_mismatch_MVAr\": round(feasibility['max_q_mismatch_MVAr'], 6),\n        \"max_voltage_violation_pu\": round(feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'], 6),\n        \"max_branch_overload_MVA\": round(feasibility['max_branch_overload_MVA'], 6),\n    }\n\n    report = {\n        \"summary\": summary,\n        \"generators\": generators,\n        \"buses\": bus_list,\n        \"most_loaded_branches\": most_loaded,\n        \"feasibility_check\": feasibility_check,\n    }\n\n    return report\n\n\ndef main():\n    # Load network data\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    print(f\"  Buses: {len(network['bus'])}\")\n    print(f\"  Generators: {len(network['gen'])}\")\n    print(f\"  Branches: {len(network['branch'])}\")\n    print(f\"  Total load: {sum(network['bus'][:, 2]):.1f} MW\")\n\n    # Solve AC OPF\n    print(\"\\nSolving AC OPF...\")\n    solution = solve_acopf(network)\n    print(f\"  Status: {solution['status']}\")\n    print(f\"  Objective: ${solution['objective']:,.2f}/hr\")\n\n    # Compute branch flows\n    print(\"\\nComputing branch flows...\")\n    branch_flows = limits\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport casadi as ca\n\ndef load_network(filepath):\n    \"\"\"Load network data from JSON.\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath) as f:\n        data = np.array(data['branch'], dtype=float),\n        'gencost': np.array(data['gencost'], dtype=float),\n    }\n\ndef build_bus_id_to_idx(buses):\n    \"\"\"Create mapping from bus ID to 0-indexed position.\"\"\"\n    return {int(buses[i, 0]): i for i in range(len(buses))}\n\ndef solve_acopf(network):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve AC OPF problem.\n\n    Returns solution dict with:\n    - Vm, Va: voltage magnitude (pu) and angle (radians)\n    - Pg, Qg: generator real/reactive power (pu)\n    - objective: total cost ($/hr)\n    - status: solver status\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    buses = network['bus']\n    gens = network['gen']\n    branches = network['branch']\n    gencost = network['gencost']\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # Build bus ID to index mapping\n    bus_id_to_idx = build_bus_id_to_idx(buses)\n\n    # Find slack bus (type 3)\n    slack_idx = None\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if buses[i, 1] == 3:\n            slack_idx = i\n            break\n\n    # Map generators to bus indices\n    gen_bus_idx = [bus_id_to_idx[int(g[0])] for g in gens]\n\n    # Filter to in-service ========== Decision Variables ==========\n    Vm = ca.MX.sym(\"Vm\", n_bus)   # Voltage magnitudes (pu)\n    Va = ca.MX.sym(\"Va\", n_bus)   # Voltage angles (radians)\n    Pg = ca.MX.sym(\"Pg\", n_gen)   # Real power (pu)\n    Qg = ca.MX.sym(\"Qg\", n_gen)   # Reactive power (pu)\n\n    # Stack all decision variables\n    x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n    # ========== Variable Bounds ==========\n    # Voltage magnitude bounds\n    Vm_min = buses[:, 12]  # VMIN\n    Vm_max = buses[:, 11]  # VMAX\n\n    # Voltage angle bounds (typically +/- pi)\n    Va_min = np.full(n_bus, -np.pi)\n    Va_max = np.full(n_bus, np.pi)\n    # Fix slack bus angle to 0\n    if slack_idx is not None:\n        Va_min[slack_idx] = 0.0\n        Va_max[slack_idx] = 0.0\n\n    # Generator bounds (convert to Pg_min[k] = gens[k, 9] / baseMVA  # PMIN\n            Pg_max[k] = gens[k, 8] / baseMVA  # PMAX\n            Qg_min[k] = gens[k, 4] / baseMVA  # QMIN\n            Qg_max[k] = gens[k, 3] / baseMVA  # c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0\n    # gencost format: [MODEL, STARTUP, SHUTDOWN, NCOST, c2, c1, c0] for polynomial\n    obj = ca.MX(0)\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        if active_gens[k]:\n            # Convert Pg from pu to MW for cost function\n            Pg_MW = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n            c2 = gencost[k, 4]  # $/MW^2\n            c1 = gencost[k, 5]  # $/MW\n            c0 = gencost[k, 6]  # $\n            obj += c2 * Pg_MW**2 + c1 * Pg_MW + c0\n\n    # ========== Constraints ==========\n    g_expr = []\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n\n    # ----- Power Balance Constraints -----\n    # For each bus: sum(Pg) - sum(Pd) - Gs*|V|^2 = sum(P_branch_out)\n    # For each bus: sum(Qg) - sum(Qd) + Bs*|V|^2 = sum(Q_branch_out)\n\n    # Aggregate generator power per bus\n    Pg_bus = [ca.MX(0) for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    Qg_bus = [ca.MX(0) for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        if active_gens[k]:\n            bus_idx = gen_bus_idx[k]\n            Pg_bus[bus_idx] += Pg[k]\n            Qg_bus[bus_idx] += Qg[k]\n\n    # Load at each bus (convert to pu)\n    Pd_bus = buses[:, 2] / baseMVA  # PD\n    Qd_bus = buses[:, 3] / baseMVA  # QD\n\n    # Shunt admittance at each bus (pu)\n    Gs_bus = buses[:, 4] / baseMVA  # GS (conductance in MW at V=1)\n    Bs_bus = buses[:, 5] / baseMVA  # BS (susceptance in MVAr at V=1)\n\n    # Compute branch flows and aggregate per bus\n    P_out = [ca.MX(0) for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    Q_out = [ca.MX(0) for _ in range(n_bus)]\n\n    # Store symbolic branch flows for limit constraints\n    S_ij_list = []  # |S| from i->j\n    S_ji_list = []  # |S| from j->i\n\n    for br_idx in range(n_branch):\n        if not active_branches[br_idx]:\n            S_ij_list.append(ca.MX(0))\n            S_ji_list.append(ca.MX(0))\n            continue\n\n        br = branches[br_idx]\n        i = branch_from_idx[br_idx]\n        j = branch_to_idx[br_idx]\n\n        br_r = br[2]   # BR_R\n        br_x = br[3]   # BR_X\n        b_c = br[4] # BR_B (total line charging)\n\n        tap = br[8]   # TAP\n        shift = br[9] # SHIFT (degrees)\n\n        # Handle tap = 0 means 1.0\n        if abs(tap) < 1e-12:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        shift_rad = np.deg2rad(shift)\n\n        # Series admittance\n        if abs(br_r) < 1e-12 and abs(br_x) < 1e-12:\n            gs = 0.0\n            bs = 0.0\n        else:\n            denom = br_r**2 + br_x**2\n            gs = br_r / denom\n            bs = -br_x / denom\n\n        inv_t = 1.0 / tap\n        inv_t2 = inv_t**2\n\n        # Voltage variables\n        Vi = Vm[i]\n        Vj = Vm[j]\n        theta_i = Va[i]\n        theta_j = Va[j]\n\n        # Angle differences\n        delta_ij = theta_i - theta_j - shift_rad\n        delta_ji = theta_j - theta_i + shift_rad\n\n        # Power flows (per-unit)\n        # P_ij = g*Vi^2*inv_t2 - Vi*Vj*inv_t*(g*cos(delta_ij) + b*sin(delta_ij))\n        P_ij = gs * Vi**2 * inv_t2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * ca.cos(delta_ij) + bs * ca.sin(delta_ij))\n        # Q_ij = -(b + b_c/2)*Vi^2*inv_t2 - Vi*Vj*inv_t*(g*sin(delta_ij) - b*cos(delta_ij))\n        Q_ij = -(bs + b_c/2) * Vi**2 * inv_t2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * ca.sin(delta_ij) - bs * ca.cos(delta_ij))\n\n        # P_ji = g*Vj^2 - Vi*Vj*inv_t*(g*cos(delta_ji) + b*sin(delta_ji))\n        P_ji = gs * Vj**2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * ca.cos(delta_ji) + bs * ca.sin(delta_ji))\n        # Q_ji = -(b + b_c/2)*Vj^2 - Vi*Vj*inv_t*(g*sin(delta_ji) - b*cos(delta_ji))\n        Q_ji = -(bs + b_c/2) * Vj**2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * ca.sin(delta_ji) - bs * ca.cos(delta_ji))\n\n        # Aggregate for nodal balance\n        P_out[i] += P_ij\n        Q_out[i] += Q_ij\n        P_out[j] += P_ji\n        Q_out[j] += Q_ji\n\n        # Apparent power magnitudes\n        S_ij = ca.sqrt(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2)\n        S_ji = ca.sqrt(P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2)\n        S_ij_list.append(S_ij)\n        S_ji_list.append(S_ji)\n\n    # Nodal balance constraints\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Real power balance\n        P_balance = Pg_bus[i] - Pd_bus[i] - Gs_bus[i] * Vm[i]**2 - P_out[i]\n        g_expr.append(P_balance)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # Reactive power balance\n        Q_balance = Qg_bus[i] - Qd_bus[i] + Bs_bus[i] * Vm[i]**2 - Q_out[i]\n        g_expr.append(Q_balance)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # ----- Branch Flow Limits -----\n    for br_idx in range(n_branch):\n        if not active_branches[br_idx]:\n            continue\n\n        rate_a = branches[br_idx, 5] / baseMVA  # RATE_A in pu\n\n        if rate_a > 1e-6:  # Only constrain if limit exists\n            # |S_ij| <= rate_a\n            g_expr.append(S_ij_list[br_idx])\n            lbg.append(-ca.inf)\n            ubg.append(rate_a)\n\n            # |S_ji| <= rate_a\n            g_expr.append(S_ji_list[br_idx])\n            lbg.append(-ca.inf)\n            ubg.append(rate_a)\n\n    # ----- Angle Difference Constraints -----\n    for br_idx in range(n_branch):\n        if not active_branches[br_idx]:\n            continue\n\n        br = branches[br_idx]\n        i = branch_from_idx[br_idx]\n        j = branch_to_idx[br_idx]\n\n        ang_min = np.deg2rad(br[11])  # ANGMIN\n        ang_max = np.deg2rad(br[12])  # ANGMAX\n\n        angle_diff = Va[i] - Va[j]\n        g_expr.append(angle_diff)\n        lbg.append(ang_min)\n        ubg.append(ang_max)\n\n    g = ca.vertcat(*g_expr)\n\n    # ========== Initialization ==========\n    # Use flat start: Vm=1.0, Va=0.0\n    Vm_init = np.ones(n_bus)\n    Va_init = np.zeros(n_bus)\n\n    # Initialize generators from data (clipped to bounds)\n    Pg_init = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    Qg_init = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        if active_gens[k]:\n            Pg_init[k] = np.clip(gens[k, 1] / baseMVA, Pg_min[k], Pg_max[k])\n            Qg_init[k] = np.clip(gens[k, 2] / baseMVA, Qg_min[k], Qg_max[k])\n\n    x0 = np.concatenate([Vm_init, Va_init, Pg_init, Qg_init]).tolist()\n\n    # ========== Create Solver ==========\n    nlp = {\"x\": x, \"f\": obj, \"g\": g}\n    opts = {\n        \"ipopt.print_level\": 0,\n        \"ipopt.max_iter\": 2000,\n        \"ipopt.tol\": 1e-7,\n        \"ipopt.acceptable_tol\": 1e-5,\n        \"ipopt.mu_strategy\": \"adaptive\",\n        \"print_time\": False,\n    }\n    solver = ca.nlpsol(\"solver\", \"ipopt\", nlp, opts)\n\n    # ========== Solve ==========\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n    # Check solver status\n    stats = solver.stats()\n    status = stats.get('return_status', 'unknown')\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = np.array(sol[\"x\"]).flatten()\n    Vm_sol = x_opt[:n_bus]\n    Va_sol = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    Pg_sol = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Qg_sol = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:]\n\n    return {\n        'Vm': Vm_sol,\n        'Va': Va_sol,\n        'Pg': Pg_sol,\n        'Qg': Qg_sol,\n        'objective': branch_to_idx,\n        'active_gens': active_gens,\n        'active_branches': active_branches,\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(Vm, Va, branches, branch_from_idx, branch_to_idx, active_branches, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute branch flows in MW/MVAr and MVA.\n\n    Returns list of dicts with flow info for each branch.\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n\n    for br_idx in Vm_max = buses[:, 11]  # VMAX\n\n    # Voltage angle bounds (typically +/- pi)\n    Va_min = np.full(n_bus, -np.pi)\n    Va_max = np.full(n_bus, np.pi)\n    # Fix slack bus angle to 0\n    if ========== Constraints ==========\n    g_expr = []\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n\n    # ----- Power Balance Constraints -----\n    # For each bus: sum(Pg) - sum(Pd) - Gs*|V|^2 = sum(P_branch_out)\n P_balance = Pg_bus[i] - Pd_bus[i] - Gs_bus[i] * Vm[i]**2 - P_out[i]\n        g_expr.append(P_balance)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # Reactive power balance\n        Q_balance = Qg_bus[i] - Qd_bus[i] + Bs_bus[i] * Vm[i]**2 - Q_out[i]\n        g_expr.append(Q_balance)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # ----- Branch Flow Limits -----\n    for br_idx in range(n_branch):\n        if not active_branches[br_idx]:\n            continue\n\n        rate_a = branches[br_idx, 5] / baseMVA  # RATE_A in pu\n\n        if rate_a > 1e-6:  # Only rate_a\n            g_expr.append(S_ji_list[br_idx])\n            lbg.append(-ca.inf)\n            ubg.append(rate_a)\n\n    # ----- Angle Difference Constraints -----\n    for br_idx in ang_min = np.deg2rad(br[11])  # ANGMIN\n        ang_max = np.deg2rad(br[12])  # ANGMAX\n\n        angle_diff = Va[i] - Va[j]\n        g_expr.append(angle_diff)\n        lbg.append(ang_min)\n        ubg.append(ang_max)\n\n    g = ca.vertcat(*g_expr)\n\n    # ========== Initialization ==========\n    # Use data-driven initialization, clipped to bounds\n    Vm_init = np.clip(buses[:, 7], Vm_min, \"ipopt.tol\": 1e-7,\n        \"ipopt.acceptable_tol\": 1e-5,\n        \"ipopt.mu_strategy\": \"adaptive\",\n        \"print_time\": False,\n    }\n    solver = ca.nlpsol(\"solver\", \"ipopt\", nlp, stats.get('return_status', 'unknown')\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = np.array(sol[\"x\"]).flatten()\n    Vm_sol = x_opt[:n_bus]\n    Va_sol = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    Pg_sol = branch_to_idx,\n        'active_gens': active_gens,\n        'active_branches': active_branches,\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(Vm, Va, branches, branch_from_idx, branch_to_idx, active_branches, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute branch flows in MW/MVAr and MVA.\n\n    Returns list of dicts with flow info for each branch.\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n\n    for br_idx in     if abs(tap) < 1e-12:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        shift_rad = np.deg2rad(shift)\n\n        if abs(br_r) < 1e-12 and abs(br_x) < 1e-12:\n            gs = 0.0\n            bs = 0.0\n        else:\n            denom = br_r**2 + br_x**2\n            gs = br_r / denom\n            bs = -br_x / denom\n\n        inv_t = 1.0 / tap\n        inv_t2 = inv_t**2\n\n        Vi = Vm[i]\n        Vj = loading_pct,\n        })\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef compute_feasibility_check(network, solution, branch_flows):\n    \"\"\"\n    Check feasibility of solution.\n\n    Returns dict with max mismatches and violations.\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    buses = network['bus']\n    gens = network['gen']\n    branches = network['branch']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['gen_bus_idx']\n    active_gens = solution['active_gens']\n    active_branches = solution['active_branches']\n    branch_from_idx = solution['branch_from_idx']\n    branch_to_idx = solution['branch_to_idx']\n\n    # Aggregate generation per bus\n    Pg_bus = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    Qg_bus = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        if active_gens[k]:\n            Pg_bus[gen_bus_idx[k]] += Pg[k]\n            Qg_bus[gen_bus_idx[k]] += Qg[k]\n\n    # Load and shunts\n    Pd_bus = buses[:, 2] / baseMVA\n    Qd_bus = buses[:, 3] / baseMVA\n    Gs_bus = buses[:, 4] / baseMVA\n    Bs_bus = buses[:, 5] / baseMVA\n\n    # Compute branch outflows\n    P_out = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    Q_out = np.zeros(n_bus)\n\n    for br_idx, br in enumerate(branches):\n        if not active_branches[br_idx]:\n            continue\n\n        i = branch_from_idx[br_idx]\n        j = branch_to_idx[br_idx]\n\n        P_out[i] += branch_flows[br_idx]['P_ij_MW'] / baseMVA\n        Q_out[i] += branch_flows[br_idx]['Q_ij_MVAr'] / baseMVA\n        P_out[j] += branch_flows[br_idx]['P_ji_MW'] / baseMVA\n        Q_out[j] += branch_flows[br_idx]['Q_ji_MVAr'] / baseMVA\n\n    # Power balance mismatches\n    P_mismatch = Pg_bus - Pd_bus - Gs_bus * Vm**2 - P_out\n    Q_mismatch = Qg_bus - Qd_bus + Bs_bus * Vm**2 - Q_out\n\n    max_p_mismatch = np.max(np.abs(P_mismatch)) * baseMVA\n    max_q_mismatch = np.max(np.abs(Q_mismatch)) * baseMVA\n\n    # Voltage violations\n    Vm_min = buses[:, 12]\n    Vm_max = buses[:, 11]\n    v_violation_low = np.maximum(0, Vm_min - Vm)\n    v_violation_high = np.maximum(0, Vm - Vm_max)\n    max_v_violation = max(np.max(v_violation_low), np.max(v_violation_high))\n\n    # Branch overloads\n    max_overload = 0.0\n    for bf in in range(len(buses))}\n\ndef solve_acopf(network):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve AC OPF problem.\n\n    Returns solution dict with:\n    - Vm, Va: voltage magnitude (pu) and angle (radians)\n    - Pg, Qg:         break\n\n    # Map generators to bus indices\n    gen_bus_idx = [bus_id_to_idx[int(g[0])] for g in gens]\n\n    # Filter to in-service generators\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7]  # GEN_STATUS\n branch_from_idx = [bus_id_to_idx[int(b[0])] for b in branches]\n    branch_to_idx = [bus_id_to_idx[int(b[1])] for b in branches]\n\n    # ========== Decision Variables ==========\n    Vm = Pg_max[k] = gens[k, 8] / baseMVA  # PMAX\n            Qg_min[k] = gens[k, 4] / baseMVA  # QMIN\n            Qg_max[k] = gens[k, 3] / baseMVA  # QMAX\n        else:\n            # Out-of-service: fix ========== Constraints ==========\n    g_expr = []\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n\n    # ----- Power Balance Constraints -----\n    # For each bus: sum(Pg) - sum(Pd) - Gs*|V|^2 = sum(P_branch_out)\n    # For each bus: sum(Qg) - sum(Qd) + Bs*|V|^2 = sum(Q_branch_out)\n\n    # Aggregate generator power per bus\n    Pg_bus = [ca.MX(0) for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    Qg_bus = [ca.MX(0) for _ in (conductance in MW at V=1)\n    Bs_bus = buses[:, 5] / baseMVA  # BS (susceptance in MVAr at V=1)\n\n    # Compute branch flows and aggregate per bus\n    P_out = [ca.MX(0) for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    Q_out = [ca.MX(0) for _ in range(n_bus)]\n\n    # Store symbolic branch flows for limit constraints\n    S_ij_list = []  # |S| from i->j\n    S_ji_list = []  # |S| from j->i\n\n    for br_idx in   # TAP\n        shift = br[9] # SHIFT (degrees)\n\n        # Handle tap = 0 means 1.0\n        if abs(tap) < 1e-12:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        shift_rad = np.deg2rad(shift)\n\n        # Series admittance\n        if abs(br_r) < 1e-12 and abs(br_x) < 1e-12:\n            gs = 0.0\n            bs = 0.0\n        else:\n            denom = br_r**2 + br_x**2\n            gs = br_r / denom\n            bs = -br_x / denom\n\n        inv_t = 1.0 / tap\n        inv_t2 = inv_t**2\n\n        # Voltage variables\n        Vi = Vm[i]\n        Vj = Vm[j]\n        theta_i = Va[i]\n        theta_j = Va[j]\n\n        # Angle differences\n        delta_ij = theta_i - theta_j - shift_rad\n        delta_ji = theta_j - theta_i + shift_rad\n\n        # Power flows (per-unit)\n        # P_ij = g*Vi^2*inv_t2 - Vi*Vj*inv_t*(g*cos(delta_ij) + b*sin(delta_ij))\n        P_ij = gs * Vi**2 * inv_t2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * ca.cos(delta_ij) + bs * ca.sin(delta_ij))\n        # Q_ij = -(b + b_c/2)*Vi^2*inv_t2 - Vi*Vj*inv_t*(g*sin(delta_ij) - b*cos(delta_ij))\n        Q_ij = -(bs + b_c/2) * Vi**2 * inv_t2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * ca.sin(delta_ij) - bs * ca.cos(delta_ij))\n\n        # P_ji = g*Vj^2 - Vi*Vj*inv_t*(g*cos(delta_ji) + b*sin(delta_ji))\n        P_ji = gs * Vj**2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * ca.cos(delta_ji) + bs * ca.sin(delta_ji))\n        # Q_ji = -(b + b_c/2)*Vj^2 - Vi*Vj*inv_t*(g*sin(delta_ji) - b*cos(delta_ji))\n        Q_ji = -(bs + b_c/2) * Vj**2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * ca.sin(delta_ji) - bs * ca.cos(delta_ji))\n\n        # Aggregate for nodal balance\n        P_out[i] += P_ij\n        Q_out[i] += Q_ij\n        P_out[j] += P_ji\n        Q_out[j] += Q_ji\n\n        # Apparent power squared (avoid sqrt for numerical stability)\n        S_ij_sq = P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2\n        S_ji_sq = P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2\n        S_ij_list.append(S_ij_sq)\n        S_ji_list.append(S_ji_sq)\n\n    # Nodal balance constraints\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Real power balance\n        P_balance = Pg_bus[i] - Pd_bus[i] - Gs_bus[i] * Vm[i]**2 - P_out[i]\n        g_expr.append(P_balance)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # Reactive power balance\n        Q_balance = Qg_bus[i] - Qd_bus[i] + Bs_bus[i] * Vm[i]**2 - Q_out[i]\n        g_expr.append(Q_balance)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # ----- Branch Flow Limits -----\n    for br_idx in range(n_branch):\n        if not active_branches[br_idx]:\n            continue\n\n        rate_a = branches[br_idx, 5] / baseMVA  # RATE_A in pu\n\n        if rate_a > 1e-6:  # Only constrain if limit exists\n            # |S_ij| <= rate_a\n            g_expr.append(S_ij_list[br_idx])\n            lbg.append(-ca.inf)\n            ubg.append(rate_a)\n\n            # |S_ji| <= rate_a\n            g_expr.append(S_ji_list[br_idx])\n            lbg.append(-ca.inf)\n            ubg.append(rate_a)\n\n    # ----- Angle Difference Constraints -----\n    for br_idx in range(n_branch):\n        if not active_branches[br_idx]:\n            continue\n\n        br = branches[br_idx]\n        i = branch_from_idx[br_idx]\n        j = branch_to_idx[br_idx]\n\n        ang_min = np.deg2rad(br[11])  # ANGMIN\n        ang_max = np.deg2rad(br[12])  # ANGMAX\n\n        angle_diff = Va[i] - Va[j]\n        g_expr.append(angle_diff)\n        lbg.append(ang_min)\n        ubg.append(ang_max)\n\n    g = ca.vertcat(*g_expr)\n\n    # ========== Initialization ==========\n    # Use data-driven initialization, clipped to bounds\n    Vm_init = np.clip(buses[:, 7], Vm_min, Vm_max)  # VM from data\n    Va_init = np.deg2rad(buses[:, 8])  # VA from data (convert to radians)\n    # Fix slack angle to 0\n    if slack_idx is not None:\n        Va_init[slack_idx] = 0.0\n\n    # Initialize generators from data (clipped to bounds)\n    Pg_init = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    Qg_init = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        if active_gens[k]:\n            Pg_init[k] = np.clip(gens[k, 1] / baseMVA, Pg_min[k], Pg_max[k])\n            Qg_init[k] = np.clip(gens[k, 2] / baseMVA, Qg_min[k], Qg_max[k])\n\n    x0 = np.concatenate([Vm_init, Va_init, Pg_init, Qg_init]).tolist()\n\n    # ========== Create Solver ==========\n    nlp = {\"x\": x, \"f\": obj, \"g\": g}\n    opts = {\n        \"ipopt.print_level\": 0,\n        \"ipopt.max_iter\": 2000,\n        \"ipopt.tol\": 1e-7,\n        \"ipopt.acceptable_tol\": 1e-5,\n        \"ipopt.mu_strategy\": \"adaptive\",\n        \"print_time\": False,\n    }\n    solver = ca.nlpsol(\"solver\", \"ipopt\", nlp, opts)\n\n    # ========== Solve ==========\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n    # Check solver status\n    stats = solver.stats()\n    status = stats.get('return_status', 'unknown')\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = np.array(sol[\"x\"]).flatten()\n    Vm_sol = x_opt[:n_bus]\n    Va_sol = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    Pg_sol = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Qg_sol = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:]\n\n    return {\n        'Vm': Vm_sol,\n        'Va': Va_sol,\n        'Pg': Pg_sol,\n        'Qg': Qg_sol,\n        'objective': float(sol[\"f\"]),\n        'status': status,\n        'bus_id_to_idx': bus_id_to_idx,\n        'gen_bus_idx': gen_bus_idx,\n        'branch_from_idx': branch_from_idx,\n        'branch_to_idx': branch_to_idx,\n        'active_gens': active_gens,\n        'active_branches': active_branches,\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(Vm, Va, branches, branch_from_idx, branch_to_idx, active_branches, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute branch flows in MW/MVAr and MVA.\n\n    Returns list of dicts with flow info for each branch.\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n\n    for br_idx in range(len(branches)):\n        br = branches[br_idx]\n        from_bus = int(br[0])\n        to_bus = int(br[1])\n\n        if not active_branches[br_idx]:\n            results.append({\n                'from_bus': from_bus,\n                'to_bus': to_bus,\n                'P_ij_MW': 0.0,\n                'Q_ij_MVAr': 0.0,\n                'S_ij_MVA': 0.0,\n                'P_ji_MW': 0.0,\n                'Q_ji_MVAr': 0.0,\n                'S_ji_MVA': 0.0,\n                'rate_a_MVA': br[5],\n                'loading_pct': 0.0,\n            })\n            continue\n\n        i = branch_from_idx[br_idx]\n        j = branch_to_idx[br_idx]\n\n        br_r = br[2]\n        br_x = br[3]\n        b_c = br[4]\n        tap = br[8]\n        shift = br[9]\n        rate_a = br[5]\n\n        if abs(tap) < 1e-12:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        shift_rad = np.deg2rad(shift)\n\n        if abs(br_r) < 1e-12 and abs(br_x) < 1e-12:\n            gs = 0.0\n            bs = 0.0\n        else:\n            denom = br_r**2 + br_x**2\n            gs = br_r / denom\n            bs = -br_x / denom\n\n        inv_t = 1.0 / tap\n        inv_t2 = inv_t**2\n\n        Vi = Vm[i]\n        Vj = Vm[j]\n        theta_i = Va[i]\n        theta_j = Va[j]\n\n        delta_ij = theta_i - theta_j - shift_rad\n        delta_ji = theta_j - theta_i + shift_rad\n\n        P_ij = gs * Vi**2 * inv_t2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * np.cos(delta_ij) + bs * np.sin(delta_ij))\n        Q_ij = -(bs + b_c/2) * Vi**2 * inv_t2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * np.sin(delta_ij) - bs * np.cos(delta_ij))\n\n        P_ji = gs * Vj**2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * np.cos(delta_ji) + bs * np.sin(delta_ji))\n        Q_ji = -(bs + b_c/2) * Vj**2 - Vi * Vj * inv_t * (gs * np.sin(delta_ji) - bs * np.cos(delta_ji))\n\n        S_ij = np.sqrt(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2)\n        S_ji = np.sqrt(P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2)\n\n        loading_pct = 0.0\n        if rate_a > 1e-6:\n            loading_pct = 100 * max(S_ij, S_ji) * baseMVA / rate_a\n\n        results.append({\n            'from_bus': from_bus,\n            'to_bus': to_bus,\n            'P_ij_MW': P_ij * baseMVA,\n            'Q_ij_MVAr': Q_ij * baseMVA,\n            'S_ij_MVA': S_ij * baseMVA,\n            'P_ji_MW': P_ji * baseMVA,\n            'Q_ji_MVAr': Q_ji * baseMVA,\n            'S_ji_MVA': S_ji * baseMVA,\n            'rate_a_MVA': rate_a,\n            'loading_pct': loading_pct,\n        })\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef compute_feasibility_check(network, solution, branch_flows):\n    \"\"\"\n    Check feasibility of solution.\n\n    Returns dict with max mismatches and violations.\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    buses = network['bus']\n    gens = network['gen']\n    branches = network['branch']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n    Pg = solution['Pg']\n    Qg = solution['Qg']\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n\n    bus_id_to_idx = solution['bus_id_to_idx']\n    gen_bus_idx = solution['gen_bus_idx']\n    active_gens = solution['active_gens']\n    active_branches = solution['active_branches']\n    branch_from_idx = solution['branch_from_idx']\n    branch_to_idx = solution['branch_to_idx']\n\n    # Aggregate generation per bus\n    Pg_bus = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    Qg_bus = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        if active_gens[k]:\n            Pg_bus[gen_bus_idx[k]] += Pg[k]\n            Qg_bus[gen_bus_idx[k]] += Qg[k]\n\n    # Load and shunts\n    Pd_bus = buses[:, 2] / baseMVA\n    Qd_bus = buses[:, 3] / baseMVA\n    Gs_bus = buses[:, 4] / baseMVA\n    Bs_bus = buses[:, 5] / baseMVA\n\n    # Compute branch outflows\n    P_out = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    Q_out = np.zeros(n_bus)\n\n    for br_idx, br in enumerate(branches):\n        if not active_branches[br_idx]:\n            continue\n\n        i = branch_from_idx[br_idx]\n        j = branch_to_idx[br_idx]\n\n        P_out[i] += branch_flows[br_idx]['P_ij_MW'] / baseMVA\n        Q_out[i] += branch_flows[br_idx]['Q_ij_MVAr'] / baseMVA\n        P_out[j] += branch_flows[br_idx]['P_ji_MW'] / baseMVA\n        Q_out[j] += branch_flows[br_idx]['Q_ji_MVAr'] / baseMVA\n\n    # Power balance mismatches\n    P_mismatch = Pg_bus - Pd_bus - Gs_bus * Vm**2 - P_out\n    Q_mismatch = Qg_bus - Qd_bus + Bs_bus * Vm**2 - Q_out\n\n    max_p_mismatch = np.max(np.abs(P_mismatch)) * baseMVA\n    max_q_mismatch = np.max(np.abs(Q_mismatch)) * baseMVA\n\n    # Voltage violations\n    Vm_min = buses[:, 12]\n    Vm_max = buses[:, 11]\n    v_violation_low = np.maximum(0, Vm_min - Vm)\n    v_violation_high = np.maximum(0, Vm - Vm_max)\n    max_v_violation = max(np.max(v_violation_low), np.max(v_violation_high))\n\n    # Branch overloads\n    max_overload = 0.0\n    for bf in branch_flows:\n        if bf['rate_a_MVA'] > 1e-6:\n            overload_ij = max(0, bf['S_ij_MVA'] - bf['rate_a_MVA'])\n            overload_ji = max(0, bf['S_ji_MVA'] - bf['rate_a_MVA'])\n            max_overload = max(max_overload, overload_ij, overload_ji)\n\n    return {\n        'max_p_mismatch_MW': max_p_mismatch,\n        'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': max_q_mismatch,\n        'max_voltage_violation_pu': max_v_violation,\n        'max_branch_overload_MVA': max_overload,\n    }\n\n\ndef generate_report(network, solution, branch_flows, feasibility):\n    \"\"\"\n    Generate report in the required JSON format.\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    buses = network['bus']\n    gens = network['gen']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n    Pg =  total_gen_MVAr = np.sum(Qg) * baseMVA\n\n    # Total losses\n    total_losses_MW = total_gen_MW - total_load_MW\n\n    # Summary\n    summary = {\n        \"total_cost_per_hour\": position.\"\"\"\n    return {int(buses[i, 0]): i for i in range(len(buses))}\n\ndef solve_acopf(network):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve AC OPF problem.\n\n    Returns solution dict with:\n    - Vm, Va: voltage magnitude (pu) and angle (radians)\n    - Pg, Qg: generator real/reactive power (pu)\n    - objective: total cost ($/hr)\n    - status: solver status\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA =    if buses[i, 1] == 3:\n            slack_idx = i\n            break\n\n    # Map generators to bus indices\n    gen_bus_idx = [bus_id_to_idx[int(g[0])] for g in gens]\n\n    # Filter to in-service
